# Crafting Chain

Generated by `Tools/SdbDocs` from a build-1962 `clientdb.sd2` (db patch prod-1962, built 2016-05-05).

Two crafting systems ship in build 1962 and they share tables. Blueprint id tells them apart:
`dbitems::Blueprint_Resources` carries ids 75430 to 85611 and nothing else, and that block is
the graded system below. Everything outside it is the v1.6 rework, a fixed ingredient list with no
stats read, and that is what [Recipes](Recipes.md) lists.

The graded system turns a resource's per-instance stats into a crafted item's attributes in five
steps. Each step below is a surviving table. The one thing that did not survive is the per-instance
stat values themselves: they were never static data. The client parses them off the item, out of a
base-36 string `{Version}-{SDB id}-{Quality}-{Stat1..Stat5}` (`lib_Items.lua`,
`LIB_ITEMS.GetResourceStats`), and the 2016 capture shows retail had stopped filling that slot.
Any server reviving this has to generate those numbers. The rest is lookup.

## 1. What a resource carries

`dbitems::ResourceStat` is the abstract index, 5 rows. Stat 5 has a localization key one past
CPU's with no row behind it, so it was authored and dropped.

| Stat | Name |
|---|---|
| 1 | Purity |
| 2 | Power |
| 3 | Mass |
| 4 | CPU |
| 5 | (never shipped) |

`dbitems::Resource_Stat_Names` gives those indices a player-facing name per material family, 28 rows.
Gas has four stats where mineral and organic have five.

| Family | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Gas (3299) | Thermal Resistance | Compressibility | Volatility | Potential Energy |   |
| Mineral (3298) | Thermal Resistance | Conductivity | Malleability | Density | Toughness |
| Organic (3300) | Thermal Resistance | Potency | Resilience | Potential Energy | Toughness |
| Raw Gas (3296) | Thermal Resistance | Compressibility | Volatility | Potential Energy |   |
| Raw Mineral (3295) | Thermal Resistance | Conductivity | Malleability | Density | Toughness |
| Raw Organic (3297) | Thermal Resistance | Potency | Resilience | Potential Energy | Toughness |

## 2. Refining

`dbitems::ResourceItem.refines_into`, 16 live pairs out of 111 rows. Refining is the step
the molecular printer did, and the printer's panel ships as blank Lua, so these are the input to a
screen v1.6 emptied. Note these are the pre-1.6 material items, not the bars a 1962 thumper pays.

| Raw | Class | Refines into | Class |
|---|---|---|---|
| Raw Copper (78014) | Raw Copper (208) | Copper^CY (77703) | Copper (133) |
| Raw Iron^CY (78015) | Raw Iron (209) | Iron^CY (77704) | Iron (134) |
| Raw Aluminum^CY (78016) | Raw Aluminum (210) | Aluminum^CY (77705) | Aluminum (135) |
| Raw Carbon^CY (78017) | Raw Carbon (211) | Carbon^CY (77706) | Carbon (136) |
| Raw Silicate^CY (78018) | Raw Silicate (212) | Silicate^CY (77707) | Silicate (137) |
| Raw Ceramic^CY (78019) | Raw Ceramic (213) | Ceramic^CY (77708) | Hybrids (138) |
| Raw Methine^CY (78020) | Raw Methine (214) | Methine^CY (77709) | Methine (139) |
| Raw Octine^CY (78021) | Raw Octine (215) | Octine^CY (77710) | Octine (140) |
| Raw Nitrine^CY (78022) | Raw Nitrine (216) | Nitrine^CY (77711) | Nitrine (141) |
| Raw Petrochemical^CY (78023) | Raw Petrochemical (217) | Petrochemical^CY (77713) | Petrochemical (142) |
| Raw Biopolymer^CY (78024) | Raw Biopolymer (218) | Biopolymer^CY (77714) | Biopolymer (143) |
| Raw Xenografts^CY (78025) | Raw Xenograft (219) | Xenografts^CY (77715) | Xenografts (144) |
| Raw Toxins^CY (78026) | Raw Toxin (220) | Toxins^CY (77716) | Toxins (145) |
| Raw Regenics^CY (78027) | Raw Regenics (221) | Regenics^CY (77736) | Regenics (146) |
| Raw Anabolics^CY (78028) | Raw Anabolics (222) | Anabolics^CY (77737) | Anabolics (147) |
| Raw Radine^CY (82420) | Raw Radine (3302) | Radine^CY (82419) | Radine (3304) |

## 3. Component recipes

A graded recipe does not name an ingredient item. It names a material *class* and which of the five
stats it reads off whatever you supply, so the same recipe run on better ore makes a better part.
`resource_stat` is that index; a row carrying `resource_stat = 0` is a slot instead, and those are
section 4.

The output is a component: 872 items across 1078 subtypes filed under `Battleframe
Components (1926)`. 817 recipes make one and read a stat while doing it.

## 4. Component slots

`dbitems::Blueprint_Attribute_Map` states the rule per equipment family: which component slot the
family has, and which attribute the part in it decides. `blueprint_type` and `component_type` are
both `Resource_Types` ids. Slots mapping to no attribute are the two that appear on every family,
`Stabilization Subroutines` and `Structural Reinforcement`.

| Equipment family | Slot | Decides |
|---|---|---|
| Accord Absorption Plating (1624) | Stabilization Subroutines (1921) |   |
| Accord Absorption Plating (1624) | Structural Reinforcement (1923) |   |
| Accord Absorption Plating (1624) | Regeneration Assembly (2056) | Item Heal |
| Accord Regenerative Plating (1882) | Stabilization Subroutines (1921) |   |
| Accord Regenerative Plating (1882) | Structural Reinforcement (1923) |   |
| Accord Regenerative Plating (1882) | Regeneration Unit (2057) | Healing |
| Accord Siphoning Plating (1708) | Stabilization Subroutines (1921) |   |
| Accord Siphoning Plating (1708) | Structural Reinforcement (1923) |   |
| Accord Siphoning Plating (1708) | Vitality Transfer System (2115) | Lifesteal |
| Adaptive Shielding (1787) | Stabilization Subroutines (1921) |   |
| Adaptive Shielding (1787) | Structural Reinforcement (1923) |   |
| Adaptive Shielding (1787) | Electron Charge Emitter (1979) | Bonus Shields |
| Adaptive Shielding (1787) | Energy Generator (1982) | Rate of Fire |

## 5. Stats to numbers

`dbitems::ItemTypeAttributeModifier` is where a component's carried stats become the number on the
item card. It is keyed by `RootItem.crafting_type_id` and gives, per attribute, a coefficient on
each of mass, power and CPU plus the range the result is held inside. `Stage` is which upgrade
stage may move it. Attributes 950 to 953 (Repair Pool, Mass, Power, CPU) appear as passthrough rows
carrying the item's own budget rather than a derived stat.

6126 rows cover 1252 crafting types. Listed here are the 2936 with at least one number on them,
across 893 crafting types; the rest carry an attribute id and nothing else, which is a row that was never
tuned rather than one that means zero.

Two shapes are visible here and they belong to different eras. Weapon rows carry fractional
coefficients that split one attribute across the three stats, and the crafting types they sit on
(Ionic, Covalent, Basalt) have no surviving items. Frame equipment rows use whole numbers in the
hundreds with a real min and max, and those crafting types do still have items.
